Discretized into bins:\\n  - 0: < 2 cm/s\\n  - 1: 2-10 cm/s\\n  - 2: 10-20 cm/s\\n  - 3: 20-40 cm/s\\n  - 4: > 40 cm/s\\n- Lick, time-varying. 0 = no, 1 = yes\\n- Reward zone location, per-trial. 0 = A, 1 = B, 2 = C\\n- Reward outcome, per-trial. 0 = no, 1 = yes\\n\\n## Target Data Format\\n\\n- Save the full converted dataset to the pickle file `converted_data.pkl`.\\n- All datasets must be converted into the following Python dictionary structure:\\n\\n```python\\ndata = {\\n    'neural': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        [  # list of trials for session 2\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'input': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'output': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\\n    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\\n\\n    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\\n    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\\n        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\\n    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\\n\\n    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\\n    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\\n    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\\n        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'metadata': {\\n        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\\n        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\\n        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\\n        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\\n        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\\n        ...\\n    }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### Key Format Requirements\\n\\n- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\\n  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\\n  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \\n  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\\n\\n- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\\n  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \\n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \\n\\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \\n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \\n\\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\\n\\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\\n\\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \\n\\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\\n\\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \\n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \\n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\\n\\n## Decoder Reference\\n\\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\\n- Optional arguments:\\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\\n\\n### Purpose\\n\\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\\n1. The reference uses a Viterbi algorithm on the `reward_zone` position data to assign zone labels.\n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Distance to reward zone*?\n\ni. Signed distance is computed: negative if before the zone, positive if past the zone, 0 if inside. Zone boundaries come from `REWARD_ZONES` dict (A: 80-130, B: 200-250, C: 320-370 cm).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position, dtype=float)\n    before = position < rz_start\n    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n    after = position > rz_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end\n    return dist\n```\n\niii. This is functionally equivalent to the reference's `compute_distance_to_reward_zone` function.\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Distance to reward zone* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Discretized into 7 bins using explicit conditional logic matching the instruction bins.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_distance_to_reward(distance):\n    out = np.zeros_like(distance, dtype=int)\n    out[distance < -50] = 0\n    out[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    out[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    out[distance == 0] = 3\n    out[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    out[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    out[distance > 50] = 6\n    return out\n```\n\niii. The bin boundaries match the instructions. The reference uses `np.digitize` with edges `[-inf, -50, -10, 0, 1e-6, 10, 50, inf]`. Both produce the same 7-bin discretization.\n\n## 7-d. Position is clipped to [0, 450] before discretization.\n\nii.\n```python\npos_clipped = np.clip(pos, 0, TRACK_LENGTH)\npos_disc = discretize_position(pos_clipped)\n```\n\niii. The reference does not clip position before discretizing. Clipping means negative positions (e.g., -500 during teleport, though these should be excluded by trial boundaries) are mapped to 0.\n\n## 8-c. How is `output` *Absolute position* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Discretized into 5 bins of 90 cm each across a 450 cm track: [0-90), [90-180), [180-270), [270-360), [360-450].\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_position(position, n_bins=5):\n    bin_size = TRACK_LENGTH / n_bins  # 450/5 = 90\n    out = np.clip(np.floor(position / bin_size).astype(int), 0, n_bins - 1)\n    return out\n```\n\niii. The AI uses 90 cm bins starting at 0. The reference uses `np.digitize` with edges `[-inf, 50, 150, 250, 350, inf]`, producing 100 cm bins centered at different positions: (<50, 50-150, 150-250, 250-350, >350). These are different bin boundaries.\n\n## 8-d. How is `output` *Absolute position* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. The AI first applies lick sensor error correction: trials where >30% of samples have lick count >2 have their lick data set to NaN. NaNs are then replaced with 0. Finally, lick values are binarized (>0 = 1).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef correct_lick_sensor_errors(lick_data, tstart_indices, teleport_indices, threshold=LICK_ERROR_THRESHOLD):\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(tstart_indices, teleport_indices)):\n        trial_licks = licks[start:end]\n        frac_high = np.sum(trial_licks > 2) / len(trial_licks)\n        if frac_high > threshold:\n            licks[start:end] = np.nan\n            error_trials.append(i)\n    return licks, error_trials\n# ...\nlck = np.nan_to_num(lck, nan=0.0)\nlck_binary = (lck > 0).astype(int)\n```\n\niii. The paper describes this lick error correction procedure. The reference code does NOT implement it, simply binarizing the raw lick data.\n\n## 9-c. How is `output` *Lick* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Same time indices as neural data.\n\nii.\n```python\nlck = licks_corrected[start:end]\n```\n\niii. Same indexing approach.\n\n## 10-a. NOT derived from the `reward_zone` time series in the data.\n\nii.\n```python\nscene = SESSIONS_INFO[gcamp_name][exp_day]\nrz_labels = parse_scene_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)\n# ...\nrz_loc = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}[rz_labels[i]]\noutput_arr[4, :] = rz_loc\n```\n\niii. Zone labels (A/B/C) are mapped to integers (0/1/2).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef parse_scene_reward_zones(scene, n_trials, change_trial=SWITCH_TRIAL):\n    if scene.endswith('_LocationA'):\n        rz_labels[:] = 'A'\n    # ... (similar for B, C)\n    else:\n        zone_before, zone_after = parse_switch_zones(scene)\n        ct = min(change_trial, n_trials)\n        rz_labels[:ct] = zone_before\n        rz_labels[ct:] = zone_after\n    return rz_labels\n```\n\niii. Zone boundaries come from `REWARD_ZONES` dict (A: 80-130, B: 200-250, C: 320-370 cm).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position, dtype=float)\n    before = position < rz_start\n    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n    after = position > rz_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end\n    return dist\n```\n\niii. Finally, lick values are binarized (>0 = 1).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef correct_lick_sensor_errors(lick_data, tstart_indices, teleport_indices, threshold=LICK_ERROR_THRESHOLD):\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(tstart_indices, teleport_indices)):\n        trial_licks = licks[start:end]\n        frac_high = np.sum(trial_licks > 2) / len(trial_licks)\n        if frac_high > threshold:\n            licks[start:end] = np.nan\n            error_trials.append(i)\n    return licks, error_trials\n# ...\nlck = np.nan_to_num(lck, nan=0.0)\nlck_binary = (lck > 0).astype(int)\n```\n\niii. log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, virtual reality (VR) navigation task (Fig. 1a–c). VR provided tight control over sensory stimuli and the animal’s trajectory, allowing us to observe neuronal remapping in relation to distant rewards, a phenomenon potentially less visible in freely moving scenarios. Furthermore, the task included multiple updates to a hidden reward zone across two environments, allowing us to dissociate spatially driven and reward-driven remapping. Imaging began on day 1 of task acquisition, in which animals traversed a unidirectional 450 cm virtual linear track (for example, Environment 1 (ENV 1)) with a hidden 50 cm reward zone where sucrose water was delivered operantly for licking (Fig. 1d,e). zone labels per trial.\\n\\n    Returns array of \\'A\\', \\'B\\', or \\'C\\' per trial.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    rz_labels = np.empty(n_trials, dtype=\\'U1\\')\\n\\n    # Determine the zone(s) from scene name\\n    # Single-zone sessions\\n    if scene.endswith(\\'_LocationA\\') or scene.endswith(\\'LocationA\\'):\\n        rz_labels[:] = \\'A\\'\\n    elif scene.endswith(\\'_LocationB\\') or scene.endswith(\\'LocationB\\'):\\n        rz_labels[:] = \\'B\\'\\n    elif scene.endswith(\\'_LocationC\\') or scene.endswith(\\'LocationC\\'):\\n        rz_labels[:] = \\'C\\'\\n    else:\\n        # Switch session: parse from->to\\n        # Patterns: \"LocationX_to_Y\", \"X_to_EnvN_Y\"\\n        # Extract the two zone letters\\n        zone_before, zone_after = parse_switch_zones(scene)\\n        ct = min(change_trial, n_trials)\\n        rz_labels[:ct] = zone_before\\n        rz_labels[ct:] = zone_after\\n\\n    return rz_labels\\n\\n\\ndef parse_switch_zones(scene):\\n    \"\"\"Parse switch session scene name to get before/after zones.\"\"\"\\n    # Patterns:\\n    # \\'Env1_LocationA_to_B\\' -> A, B\\n    # \\'Env1_LocationA_to_C\\' -> A, C\\n    # \\'Env1_A_to_Env2_B\\' -> A, B (environment switch)\\n    # \\'Env1_B_to_Env2_C\\' -> B, C\\n    # \\'Env2_B_to_Env1_A\\' -> B, A\\n\\n    scene_upper = scene.upper()\\n\\n    # Find \\'TO\\' separator\\n    if \\'_TO_\\' in scene_upper:\\n        parts = scene.split(\\'_to_\\')\\n        if len(parts) != 2:\\n            parts = scene.split(\\'_to_\\')\\n\\n        before_part = parts[0]\\n        after_part = parts[1]\\n\\n        # Extract zone letter from before part (last character of Location*)\\n        zone_before = None\\n        for z in [\\'A\\', \\'B\\', \\'C\\']:\\n            if before_part.endswith(z) or f\\'Location{z}\\' in before_part or f\\'_{z}_\\' in before_part:\\n                zone_before = z\\n                break\\n        if zone_before is None:\\n            # Try pattern like \\'Env1_C_to_Env2_A\\'\\n            for z in [\\'A\\', \\'B\\', \\'C\\']:\\n                if f\\'_{z}\\' in before_part:\\n                    zone_before = z\\n\\n        # Extract zone letter from after part\\n        zone_after = None\\n        for z in [\\'A\\', \\'B\\', \\'C\\']:\\n            if after_part.endswith(z) or f\\'Location{z}\\' in after_part or z == after_part[-1]:\\n                zone_after = z\\n                break\\n        if zone_after is None:\\n            for z in [\\'A\\', \\'B\\', \\'C\\']:\\n                if z in after_part:\\n                    zone_after = z\\n\\n        if zone_before and zone_after:\\n            return zone_before, zone_after\\n\\n    raise ValueError(f\"Cannot parse switch zones from scene: {scene}\")\\n\\n\\ndef parse_scene_environment(scene):\\n    \"\"\"Determine environment type from scene name.\\n    Returns 0 for ENV1, 1 for ENV2.\\n    For switch sessions with env change (day 8), returns (env_before, env_after).\\n    \"\"\"\\n    if \\'Env1\\' in scene and \\'Env2\\' not in scene:\\n        return 0, None\\n    elif \\'Env2\\' in scene and \\'Env1\\' not in scene:\\n        return 1, None\\n    elif \\'Env1\\' in scene and \\'Env2\\' in scene:\\n        # Environment switch (day 8)\\n        # e.g., \\'Env1_A_to_Env2_B\\' or \\'Env2_B_to_Env1_A\\'\\n        parts = scene.split(\\'_to_\\')\\n        env_before = 0 if \\'Env1\\' in parts[0] else 1\\n        env_after = 0 if \\'Env1\\' in parts[1] else 1\\n        return env_before, env_after\\n    else:\\n        return 0, None  # default\\n\\n\\ndef get_reward_zone_coords(zone_label):\\n    \"\"\"Get (start, end) coordinates for a reward zone label.\"\"\"\\n    return REWARD_ZONES[zone_label]\\n\\n\\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\\n    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\\n\\n    Negative = before reward zone, positive = after reward zone, 0 = inside.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position, dtype=float)\\n    before = position < rz_start\\n    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\\n    after = position > rz_end\\n\\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start\\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end\\n\\n    return dist\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_distance_to_reward(distance):\\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone.\\n    Bins:\\n      0: < -50 cm\\n      1: -50 to -10 cm\\n      2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\\n      3: 0 cm (inside zone)\\n      4: >0 cm to +10 cm\\n      5: +10 to +50 cm\\n      6: > +50 cm\\n    \"\"\"\\n    out = np.zeros_like(distance, dtype=int)\\n    out[distance < -50] = 0\\n    out[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\\n    out[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\\n    out[distance == 0] = 3\\n    out[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\\n    out[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\\n    out[distance > 50] = 6\\n    return out\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_position(position, n_bins=5):\\n    \"\"\"Discretize position into n_bins equal-sized bins across 0-450 cm.\\n    Bins: 0-90, 90-180, 180-270, 270-360, 360-450\\n    \"\"\"\\n    bin_size = TRACK_LENGTH / n_bins\\n    out = np.clip(np.floor(position / bin_size).astype(int), 0, n_bins - 1)\\n    return out\\n\\n\\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed.\\n    Bins:\\n      0: < 2 cm/s\\n      1: 2-10 cm/s\\n      2: 10-20 cm/s\\n      3: 20-40 cm/s\\n      4: > 40 cm/s\\n    \"\"\"\\n    out = np.zeros_like(speed, dtype=int)\\n    out[speed < 2] = 0\\n    out[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\\n    out[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\\n    out[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\\n    out[speed >= 40] = 4\\n    return out\\n\\n\\ndef correct_lick_sensor_errors(lick_data, tstart_indices, teleport_indices, threshold=LICK_ERROR_THRESHOLD):\\n    \"\"\"Correct lick sensor errors per the paper\\'s method.\\n\\n    Trials where >threshold fraction of samples have cumulative lick count >2\\n    are set to NaN.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    licks = np.copy(lick_data)\\n    error_trials = []\\n\\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(tstart_indices, teleport_indices)):\\n        trial_licks = licks[start:end]\\n        if len(trial_licks) == 0:\\n            continue\\n        frac_high = np.sum(trial_licks > 2) / len(trial_licks)\\n        if frac_high > threshold:\\n            licks[start:end] = np.nan\\n            error_trials.append(i)\\n\\n    return licks, error_trials\\n\\n\\ndef identify_interneurons(neural_data, speed_data, valid_mask, threshold=SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD):\\n    \"\"\"Identify putative interneurons by correlation with running speed.\\n\\n    Per the paper: Pearson correlation > 0.5 between dF/F and speed.\\n    We use the deconvolved activity here as we don\\'t have separate dF/F,\\n    but the NWB files contain fluorescence which we can use instead.\\n\\n    Returns boolean mask where True = interneuron.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    n_neurons = neural_data.shape[1]\\n    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_neurons, dtype=bool)\\n\\n    for c in range(n_neurons):\\n        neural_ts = neural_data[valid_mask, c]\\n        speed_ts = speed_data[valid_mask]\\n\\n        if np.std(neural_ts) == 0 or np.std(speed_ts) == 0:\\n            continue\\n\\n        r = np.corrcoef(neural_ts, speed_ts)[0, 1]\\n        if r > threshold:\\n            is_interneuron[c] = True\\n\\n    return is_interneuron\\n\\n\\ndef load_nwb_session(filepath):\\n    \"\"\"Load data from a single NWB file.\"\"\"\\n    with h5py.File(filepath, \\'r\\') as f:\\n        bts = f[\\'processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries\\']\\n        ophys = f[\\'processing/ophys\\']\\n        seg = f[\\'processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation\\']\\n\\n        # Behavioral data\\n        position = bts[\\'position/data\\'][:]\\n        speed = bts[\\'speed/data\\'][:]\\n        lick = bts[\\'lick/data\\'][:]\\n        trial_start = bts[\\'trial_start/data\\'][:]\\n        teleport = bts[\\'teleport/data\\'][:]\\n        trial_num = bts[\\'trial number/data\\'][:]\\n        environment = bts[\\'environment/data\\'][:]\\n        reward_zone = bts[\\'reward_zone/data\\'][:]\\n        timestamps = bts[\\'position/timestamps\\'][:]\\n        autoreward = bts[\\'autoreward/data\\'][:]\\n\\n        # Reward events (separate timestamps)\\n        reward_data = bts[\\'Reward/data\\'][:]\\n        reward_ts = bts[\\'Reward/timestamps\\'][:]\\n\\n        # ROI info\\n        iscell = seg[\\'iscell\\'][:]\\n        plane_idx = seg[\\'planeIdx\\'][:]\\n\\n        # Neural data - handle multi-plane\\n        deconv_keys = list(ophys[\\'Deconvolved\\'].keys())\\n        fluor_keys = list(ophys[\\'Fluorescence\\'].keys())\\n\\n        # Concatenate planes (paper says \"ROIs were identified separately per plane,\\n        # but planes were pooled for all analyses\")\\n        deconv_list = []\\n        fluor_list = []\\n        for pk in sorted(deconv_keys):\\n            deconv_list.append(ophys[\\'Deconvolved\\'][pk][\\'data\\'][:])\\n        for fk in sorted(fluor_keys):\\n            fluor_list.append(ophys[\\'Fluorescence\\'][fk][\\'data\\'][:])\\n\\n        deconvolved = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\\n        fluorescence = np.concatenate(fluor_list, axis=1)\\n\\n        # Imaging rate\\n        imaging_rate = f[\\'acquisition/TwoPhotonSeries/imaging_plane/imaging_rate\\'][()]\\n\\n        # Subject info\\n        subject_id = f[\\'general/subject/subject_id\\'][()]\\n        if isinstance(subject_id, bytes):\\n            subject_id = subject_id.decode()\\n        session_id = f[\\'general/session_id\\'][()]\\n        if isinstance(session_id, bytes):\\n            session_id = session_id.decode()\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\'position\\': position,\\n        \\'speed\\': speed,\\n        \\'lick\\': lick,\\n        \\'trial_start\\': trial_start,\\n        \\'teleport\\': teleport,\\n        \\'trial_num\\': trial_num,\\n        \\'environment\\': environment,\\n        \\'reward_zone_ts\\': reward_zone,\\n        \\'timestamps\\': timestamps,\\n        \\'autoreward\\': autoreward,\\n        \\'reward_data\\': reward_data,\\n        \\'reward_ts\\': reward_ts,\\n        \\'iscell\\': iscell,\\n        \\'plane_idx\\': plane_idx,\\n        \\'deconvolved\\': deconvolved,\\n        \\'fluorescence\\': fluorescence,\\n        \\'imaging_rate\\': imaging_rate,\\n        \\'subject_id\\': subject_id,\\n        \\'session_id\\': session_id,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef process_session(nwb_data, scene, exp_day):\\n    \"\"\"Process a single session into trials.\\n\\n    Returns dict with neural, input, output arrays per trial,\\n    plus metadata.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    # Get trial boundaries\\n    tstart_idx = np.where(nwb_data[\\'trial_start\\'] > 0)[0]\\n    teleport_idx = np.where(nwb_data[\\'teleport\\'] > 0)[0]\\n\\n    n_trials = min(len(tstart_idx), len(teleport_idx))\\n    tstart_idx = tstart_idx[:n_trials]\\n    teleport_idx = teleport_idx[:n_trials]\\n\\n    if n_trials < 2:\\n        print(f\"  Skipping session: only {n_trials} trials\")\\n        return None\\n\\n    # Filter neurons by iscell (manual curation from Suite2P)\\n    cell_mask = nwb_data[\\'iscell\\'][:, 0] == 1\\n    n_total_rois = len(cell_mask)\\n    n_cells_after_iscell = cell_mask.sum()\\n\\n    # Get neural data for valid cells\\n    deconvolved = nwb_data[\\'deconvolved\\'][:, cell_mask]\\n    fluorescence = nwb_data[\\'fluorescence\\'][:, cell_mask]\\n\\n    # Identify and exclude putative interneurons using fluorescence (dF/F proxy)\\n    # Per the paper: correlation of dF/F with running speed > 0.5\\n    # Use non-NaN timepoints where animal is on the track\\n    valid_mask = nwb_data[\\'position\\'] > 0  # exclude teleport period (pos = -500)\\n\\n    is_interneuron = identify_interneurons(\\n        fluorescence, nwb_data[\\'speed\\'], valid_mask,\\n        threshold=SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD\\n    )\\n\\n    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\\n    neuron_mask = ~is_interneuron\\n    n_neurons = neuron_mask.sum()\\n\\n    if n_neurons < 1:\\n        print(f\"  Skipping session: no valid neurons\")\\n        return None\\n\\n    # Apply neuron mask\\n    neural = deconvolved[:, neuron_mask]  # (timepoints, n_neurons)\\n\\n    # Correct lick sensor errors\\n    licks_corrected, error_trials = correct_lick_sensor_errors(\\n        nwb_data[\\'lick\\'], tstart_idx, teleport_idx,\\n        threshold=LICK_ERROR_THRESHOLD\\n    )\\n\\n    # Determine reward zone labels per trial\\n    rz_labels = parse_scene_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)\\n\\n    # Determine environment type per trial\\n    env_before, env_after = parse_scene_environment(scene)\\n    env_per_trial = np.full(n_trials, env_before, dtype=int)\\n    if env_after is not None:\\n        # Environment switch session\\n        ct = min(SWITCH_TRIAL, n_trials)\\n        env_per_trial[ct:] = env_after\\n\\n    # Determine reward outcome per trial\\n    reward_per_trial = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\\n    timestamps = nwb_data[\\'timestamps\\']\\n    reward_ts = nwb_data[\\'reward_ts\\']\\n\\n    for i in range(n_trials):\\n        start_t = timestamps[tstart_idx[i]]\\n        end_t = timestamps[teleport_idx[i]]\\n        if np.any((reward_ts >= start_t) & (reward_ts <= end_t)):\\n            reward_per_trial[i] = 1\\n\\n    # Previous trial outcome (first trial has no previous, set to 0)\\n    prev_outcome = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\\n    prev_outcome[1:] = reward_per_trial[:-1]\\n\\n    # Frame time\\n    frame_time = 1.0 / nwb_data[\\'imaging_rate\\']\\n\\n    # Process each trial\\n    trial_neural = []\\n    trial_input = []\\n    trial_output = []\\n    valid_trial_indices = []\\n\\n    for i in range(n_trials):\\n        start = tstart_idx[i]\\n        end = teleport_idx[i]\\n\\n        if end <= start:\\n            continue\\n\\n        n_timepoints = end - start\\n        if n_timepoints < 2:\\n            continue\\n\\n        # Neural data: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\\n        trial_n = neural[start:end, :].T.astype(np.float32)\\n\\n        # Position and speed for this trial\\n        pos = nwb_data[\\'position\\'][start:end]\\n        spd = np.abs(nwb_data[\\'speed\\'][start:end])  # use absolute speed\\n        lck = licks_corrected[start:end]\\n\\n        # Handle NaN licks (from error correction) - set to 0\\n        lck = np.nan_to_num(lck, nan=0.0)\\n        # Binarize licks: >0 = 1\\n        lck_binary = (lck > 0).astype(int)\\n\\n        # Time from start of trial (seconds)\\n        time_from_start = (np.arange(n_timepoints) * frame_time).astype(np.float32)\\n\\n        # ---- INPUTS ----\\n        # 1. Time from start of trial (continuous)\\n        # 2. Environment type (binary, per trial)\\n        # 3. Trial number (continuous, per trial)\\n        # 4. Previous trial outcome (binary, per trial)\\n\\n        input_arr = np.array([\\n            time_from_start[0] if False else 0,  # placeholder\\n        ])\\n        # Actually, inputs can be time-varying or per-trial\\n        # Time from start is time-varying, rest are per-trial\\n        # Shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\\n        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\\n        # Let\\'s make time_from_start time-varying and others scalar\\n\\n        input_time_varying = time_from_start  # (n_timepoints,)\\n        input_per_trial = np.array([\\n            float(env_per_trial[i]),\\n            float(i),  # trial number\\n            float(prev_outcome[i]),\\n        ], dtype=np.float32)\\n\\n        # Combine: first row is time-varying, rest are repeated per trial\\n        input_arr = np.zeros((4, n_timepoints), dtype=np.float32)\\n        input_arr[0, :] = time_from_start\\n        input_arr[1, :] = env_per_trial[i]\\n        input_arr[2, :] = float(i)\\n        input_arr[3, :] = float(prev_outcome[i])\\n\\n        # ---- OUTPUTS ----\\n        # 1. Distance to reward zone (discretized, time-varying)\\n        rz_start, rz_end = get_reward_zone_coords(rz_labels[i])\\n        dist = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(pos, rz_start, rz_end)\\n        dist_disc = discretize_distance_to_reward(dist)\\n\\n        # 2. Absolute position (discretized into 5 bins, time-varying)\\n        pos_clipped = np.clip(pos, 0, TRACK_LENGTH)\\n        pos_disc = discretize_position(pos_clipped)\\n\\n        # 3. Speed (discretized, time-varying)\\n        speed_disc = discretize_speed(spd)\\n\\n        # 4. Lick (binary, time-varying)\\n\\n        # 5. Reward zone location (per trial): 0=A, 1=B, 2=C\\n        rz_loc = {\\'A\\': 0, \\'B\\': 1, \\'C\\': 2}[rz_labels[i]]\\n\\n        # 6. Reward outcome (per trial): 0=no, 1=yes\\n\\n        output_arr = np.zeros((6, n_timepoints), dtype=np.int64)\\n        output_arr[0, :] = dist_disc\\n        output_arr[1, :] = pos_disc\\n        output_arr[2, :] = speed_disc\\n        output_arr[3, :] = lck_binary\\n        output_arr[4, :] = rz_loc\\n        output_arr[5, :] = reward_per_trial[i]\\n\\n        trial_neural.append(trial_n)\\n        trial_input.append(input_arr)\\n        trial_output.append(output_arr)\\n        valid_trial_indices.append(i)\\n\\n    if len(trial_neural) < 2:\\n        print(f\"  Skipping session: only {len(trial_neural)} valid trials\")\\n        return None\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\'neural\\': trial_neural,\\n        \\'input\\': trial_input,\\n        \\'output\\': trial_output,\\n        \\'n_neurons\\': n_neurons,\\n        \\'n_total_rois\\': n_total_rois,\\n        \\'n_cells_after_iscell\\': n_cells_after_iscell,\\n        \\'n_interneurons\\': n_interneurons,\\n        \\'n_trials\\': len(trial_neural),\\n        \\'n_lick_error_trials\\': len(error_trials),\\n        \\'rz_labels\\': rz_labels[valid_trial_indices],\\n        \\'env_per_trial\\': env_per_trial[valid_trial_indices],\\n        \\'reward_per_trial\\': reward_per_trial[valid_trial_indices],\\n        \\'scene\\': scene,\\n        \\'exp_day\\': exp_day,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef convert_all_data(data_dir, sample_only=False):\\n    \"\"\"Convert all NWB files to the decoder format.\"\"\"\\n\\n    subjects = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir) if d.startswith(\\'sub-\\')])\\n\\n    all_neural = []\\n    all_input = []\\n    all_output = []\\n    all_subject_idx = []\\n    all_brain_region_idx = []\\n\\n    subject_names = []\\n    session_info_list = []\\n\\n    total_sessions = 0\\n    total_trials = 0\\n    total_neurons_list = []\\n\\n    for subj_dir_name in subjects:\\n        subj_path = os.path.join(data_dir, subj_dir_name)\\n        subj_id = subj_dir_name  # e.g., \\'sub-m11\\'\\n\\n        if subj_id not in SUBJECT_MAP:\\n            print(f\"Warning: Unknown subject {subj_id}, skipping\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        gcamp_name = SUBJECT_MAP[subj_id]\\n        mouse_name = subj_id.replace(\\'sub-\\', \\'\\')  # e.g., \\'m11\\'\\n\\n        if mouse_name not in subject_names:\\n            subject_names.append(mouse_name)\\n        subj_idx = subject_names.index(mouse_name)\\n\\n        session_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(subj_path) if f.endswith(\\'.nwb\\')])\\n\\n        if sample_only:\\n            session_files = session_files[:2]\\n\\n        for sess_file in session_files:\\n            filepath = os.path.join(subj_path, sess_file)\\n\\n            # Extract exp_day from filename\\n            # e.g., \\'sub-m11_ses-03_behavior+ophys.nwb\\' -> ses-03 -> 3\\n            ses_part = sess_file.split(\\'_\\')[1]  # \\'ses-03\\'\\n            exp_day = int(ses_part.split(\\'-\\')[1])\\n\\n            # Get scene from sessions info\\n            if gcamp_name not in SESSIONS_INFO:\\n                print(f\"Warning: No session info for {gcamp_name}, skipping\")\\n                continue\\n            if exp_day not in SESSIONS_INFO[gcamp_name]:\\n                print(f\"Warning: No session info for {gcamp_name} day {exp_day}, skipping\")\\n                continue\\n\\n            scene = SESSIONS_INFO[gcamp_name][exp_day]\\n\\n            print(f\"Processing {subj_id} ses-{exp_day:02d} ({scene})...\")\\n\\n            try:\\n                nwb_data = load_nwb_session(filepath)\\n                result = process_session(nwb_data, scene, exp_day)\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                print(f\"  Error: {e}\")\\n                import traceback\\n                traceback.print_exc()\\n                continue\\n\\n            if result is None:\\n                continue\\n\\n            all_neural.append(result[\\'neural\\'])\\n            all_input.append(result[\\'input\\'])\\n            all_output.append(result[\\'output\\'])\\n            all_subject_idx.append(subj_idx)\\n            all_brain_region_idx.append(np.zeros(result[\\'n_neurons\\'], dtype=int))\\n\\n            session_info_list.append({\\n                \\'subject\\': mouse_name,\\n                \\'gcamp_name\\': gcamp_name,\\n                \\'exp_day\\': exp_day,\\n                \\'scene\\': scene,\\n                \\'n_neurons\\': result[\\'n_neurons\\'],\\n                \\'n_total_rois\\': result[\\'n_total_rois\\'],\\n                \\'n_cells_after_iscell\\': result[\\'n_cells_after_iscell\\'],\\n                \\'n_interneurons\\': result[\\'n_interneurons\\'],\\n                \\'n_trials\\': result[\\'n_trials\\'],\\n                \\'n_lick_error_trials\\': result[\\'n_lick_error_trials\\'],\\n            })\\n\\n            total_sessions += 1\\n            total_trials += result[\\'n_trials\\']\\n            total_neurons_list.append(result[\\'n_neurons\\'])\\n\\n            print(f\"  {result[\\'n_neurons\\']} neurons, {result[\\'n_trials\\']} trials \"\\n                  f\"({result[\\'n_interneurons\\']} interneurons excluded, \"\\n                  f\"{result[\\'n_lick_error_trials\\']} lick error trials)\")\\n\\n    # Build the output dictionary\\n    data = {\\n        \\'neural\\': all_neural,\\n        \\'input\\': all_input,\\n        \\'output\\': all_output,\\n\\n        \\'subjects\\': subject_names,\\n        \\'subject_idx\\': np.array(all_subject_idx, dtype=int),\\n\\n        \\'brain_regions\\': [\\'CA1\\'],\\n        \\'brain_region_idx\\': all_brain_region_idx,\\n\\n        \\'input_names\\': [\\n            \\'time_from_trial_start\\',\\n            \\'environment_type\\',\\n            \\'trial_number\\',\\n            \\'previous_trial_outcome\\',\\n        ],\\n\\n Plitt & Giocomo (2025), \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward.\"\\n\\n2-photon calcium imaging of CA1 neurons in head-fixed mice performing a virtual reality (VR) navigation task with hidden reward zones.\\n\\n## Task Description\\n\\nMice navigate a 450 cm virtual linear track with a hidden 50 cm reward zone at one of three possible locations:\\n- Zone A: 80-130 cm\\n- Zone B: 200-250 cm\\n- Zone C: 320-370 cm\\n\\nReward zone switches occur after 30 trials on designated days. Two visually distinct environments (ENV1, ENV2) are used across 14 days. Reward is randomly omitted on ~15% of trials.\\n\\n## Data Format\\n\\nSee `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` for detailed documentation of the conversion process.\\n\\n### Decoder Inputs (4 dimensions)\\n1. **time_from_trial_start** - seconds from trial onset (time-varying)\\n2. **environment_type** - 0=ENV1, 1=ENV2 (per-trial)\\n3. **trial_number** - trial index within session (per-trial)\\n4. **previous_trial_outcome** - 0=omitted, 1=rewarded (per-trial)\\n\\n### Decoder Outputs (6 dimensions)\\n1. **distance_to_reward_zone** - signed distance discretized into 7 bins\\n2. **absolute_position** - track position in 5 equal bins (90 cm each)\\n3. **speed** - running speed in 5 bins\\n4. **lick** - binary licking indicator\\n5. **reward_zone_location** - 0=A, 1=B, 2=C (per-trial)\\n6. **reward_outcome** - 0=no, 1=yes (per-trial)\\n\\n## Files\\n\\n- `convert_data.py` - Conversion script\\n- `converted_data.pkl` - Full converted dataset (152 sessions, 12,216 trials)\\n- `sample_data.pkl` - Sample dataset (2 sessions per subject)\\n- `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` - Detailed conversion documentation\\n- `train_decoder.py` / `decoder.py` - Decoder training scripts\\n\\n## Usage\\n\\n```bash\\n# Convert data\\npython convert_data.py --data-dir data --output converted_data.pkl\\n\\n# Convert sample\\npython convert_data.py --sample\\n\\n# Verify format\\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --verify-only\\n\\n# Train decoder\\npython train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --cpu\\n```\\n\\n## Statistics\\n\\n- 11 subjects (switch task mice)\\n- 152 sessions (12-14 per subject)\\n- 12,216 total trials (mean 80.4 per session)\\n- 155-2,337 neurons per session (mean 911)\\n- ~15.5 Hz imaging rate (~64.5 ms time bins)\\n- ~15% reward omission rate\\n'}\n--- step 64 observation ---\nFile created successfully at: /app/README.md\n\n[metadata] {\"type\": \"create\", \"filePath\": \"/app/README.md\", \"content\": \"# Neural Decoder: Hippocampal CA1 VR Navigation Task\\n\\n## Dataset\\n\\nData from Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025), \\\"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward.\\\"\\n\\n2-photon calcium imaging of CA1 neurons in head-fixed mice performing a virtual reality (VR) navigation task with hidden reward zones.\\n\\n## Task Description\\n\\nMice navigate a 450 cm virtual linear track with a hidden 50 cm reward zone at one of three possible locations:\\n- Zone A: 80-130 cm\\n- Zone B: 200-250 cm\\n- Zone C: 320-370 cm\\n\\nReward zone switches occur after 30 trials on designated days. Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025), \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\"\\n- **Data format**: NWB files from DANDI archive\\n- **Subjects**: 11 mice (switch task), ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m4 | GCAMP4 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m7 | GCAMP7 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m11 | GCAMP11 | ses-03 to ses-14 | Single plane, imaging started day 3 |\\n| sub-m12 | GCAMP12 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m13 | GCAMP13 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m14 | GCAMP14 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m15 | GCAMP15 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n| sub-m17 | GCAMP17 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Multi-plane (2 planes, pooled) |\\n| sub-m18 | GCAMP18 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Multi-plane (2 planes, pooled) |\\n| sub-m19 | GCAMP19 | ses-01 to ses-14 | Single plane |\\n\\nNote: 3 \"fixed-condition\" mice (GCAMP2, GCAMP6, GCAMP10) are not in the NWB dataset.\\n\\n## Neural Data Processing\\n\\n### ROI Selection\\n1. **Suite2P curation**: Only ROIs with `iscell[:, 0] == 1` are included (manual curation)\\n2. **Interneuron exclusion**: ROIs with Pearson correlation > 0.5 between fluorescence (dF/F) and running speed are excluded as putative interneurons (per Methods: \"Pearson correlation of >0.5 between dF/F timeseries and the animal\\'s running speed\")\\n\\n### Neural Signal\\n- Used **deconvolved calcium activity** from NWB files (`processing/ophys/Deconvolved`)\\n- This corresponds to the OASIS-deconvolved signal described in the paper\\n- For multi-plane animals (m17, m18), planes are pooled per the paper: \"ROIs were identified separately per plane, but planes were pooled for all analyses\"\\n\\n### Dimension Alignment\\n- One session (m18 ses-01) had a 1-sample mismatch between behavioral and neural data; truncated to minimum length\\n\\n## Behavioral Data Processing\\n\\n### Trial Segmentation\\n- Trials defined by `trial_start` and `teleport` events in the NWB behavioral timeseries\\n- Each trial spans from trial_start to teleport (the on-track portion only, excluding teleport zone)\\n\\n### Lick Sensor Error Correction\\n- Per the paper: trials where >30% of imaging frame samples have cumulative lick count >2 are flagged\\n- Lick data for error trials is set to 0 (not NaN) after correction\\n- Remaining lick counts binarized: >0 = lick, 0 = no lick\\n\\n### Speed\\n- Absolute value of speed is used\\n- Speed discretized per specifications\\n\\n### Reward Determination\\n- Reward events have separate timestamps in the NWB file\\n- A trial is \"rewarded\" if any reward timestamp falls within the trial boundaries\\n- Previous trial outcome for the first trial of each session is set to 0\\n\\n## Reward Zone Determination\\n\\nReward zone locations per trial are determined from the session scene metadata (from `sessions_dict.py`):\\n- **Zone A**: 80-130 cm\\n- **Zone B**: 200-250 cm\\n- **Zone C**: 320-370 cm\\n\\nOn switch days, trials 0-29 use the pre-switch zone and trials 30+ use the post-switch zone (per paper: \"On day 3, the reward zone was moved after 30 trials\").\\n\\n## Environment Type\\n\\nDetermined from scene name:\\n- Sessions with \"Env1\" only: environment = 0\\n- Sessions with \"Env2\" only: environment = 1\\n- Day 8 sessions (environment switch): first 30 trials = original env, remaining = new env\\n\\n## Temporal Alignment\\n\\n- Aligned to **start of trial** (trial_start event)\\n- Time bin size: ~64.48 ms (1/15.5078125 Hz imaging rate)\\n- `off_start = 0.0` (alignment event is the trial start itself)\\n- `off_end = None` (variable trial lengths due to running speed and teleport jitter)\\n\\n## Decoder Variables\\n\\n### Inputs (4 dimensions, all time-varying)\\n1. **time_from_trial_start**: seconds, continuous\\n2. **environment_type**: 0 or 1, constant within trial (except day 8 switch)\\n3. **trial_number**: 0-indexed trial within session, constant within trial\\n4. **previous_trial_outcome**: 0=omitted, 1=rewarded, constant within trial\\n\\n### Outputs (6 dimensions)\\n1. **distance_to_reward_zone** (7 bins, time-varying):\\n   - 0: < -50 cm, 1: -50 to -10 cm, 2: -10 to 0 cm\\n   - 3: 0 cm (inside zone), 4: 0 to +10 cm\\n   - 5: +10 to +50 cm, 6: > +50 cm\\n2. **absolute_position** (5 bins, time-varying):\\n   - 0: 0-90 cm, 1: 90-180, 2: 180-270, 3: 270-360, 4: 360-450\\n3. **speed** (5 bins, time-varying):\\n   - 0: <2, 1: 2-10, 2: 10-20, 3: 20-40, 4: >40 cm/s\\n4. **lick** (2 bins, time-varying): 0=no, 1=yes\\n5. **reward_zone_location** (3 bins, per-trial): 0=A, 1=B, 2=C\\n6. **reward_outcome** (2 bins, per-trial): 0=no, 1=yes\\n\\n## Validation Results\\n\\n### Data Statistics (matching paper)\\n| Metric | Converted | Paper |\\n|--------|-----------|-------|\\n| N subjects | 11 | 11 switch mice |\\n| N sessions | 152 | ~154 (14 days x 11 mice, minus m11 days 1-2) |\\n| Trials/session | 80.4 +/- 6.1 | 80.5 +/- 7.4 |\\n| Neurons/session | 155-2337 | 155-2172 |\\n| Omission rate | 15.3% | ~15% |\\n| Imaging rate | 15.5 Hz | ~15.5 Hz |\\n\\n### Decoder Performance (validation balanced accuracy)\\n| Output | Accuracy | Chance |\\n|--------|----------|--------|\\n| distance_to_reward_zone | 0.389 | 0.143 |\\n| absolute_position | 0.510 | 0.200 |\\n| speed | 0.384 | 0.200 |\\n| lick | 0.639 | 0.500 |\\n| reward_zone_location | 0.800 | 0.333 |\\n| reward_outcome | 0.510 | 0.500 |\\n\\nAll outputs decoded well above chance level.\\n\\n### Notes on Neuron Count Discrepancy\\n- Paper reports max
